E1360     D0.INTRO
          Section: D            Level: Household          CAI Reference: Q1360
          Type: Numeric         Width: 1                  Decimals: 0

          D0.
          IF Q376 IS (NE 2)
             Families and friends often help one another in different ways. Part of
          our
             research is finding out how they do that.
             IF Q513 IS (GT0)
                The next questions are about your family, beginning with your (or your
                husband's/or your wife's/or your partner's/.../or your late
          husband's/or
                your late wife's/or your late partner's/or your late
                (husband's/partner's)/or your late (wife's/partner's)) children.
             ELSE
                The next questions are about your family.
             END
          ELSE
             Now a few questions about your family.
          END

          User note: This preamble variable has been included in this data set in
          order to document questionnaire flow; all data values are blanks.

E1666_1   D145_.CKPT PARENTS ALIVE
          Section: D            Level: Household          CAI Reference: Q1666
          Type: Numeric         Width: 1                  Decimals: 0

          User note:  For E1666_1 for previously interviewed respondents, the variable
          indicates whether their parents were alive at the time of the previous
          interview; for new respondents, whether their parents are alive at the time
          of this interview.  For E1666_2 for previously interviewed respondents who
          have a new spouse or partner as of this wave, and whose spouse or partner,
          if any, at the time of the previous interview is not dead, the variable
          indicates whether the parents of their current spouse or partner are alive
          at the time of this interview; for all other previously interviewed
          respondents who have a spouse or partner at the time of this interview, the
          variable indicates whether the parents of their spouse or partner at time of
          their previous interview were alive at the time of that interview.  However,
          see also E1666_2A.

E1666_2   D145.CKPT PARENTS-IN-LAW ALIVE
          Section: D            Level: Household          CAI Reference: Q1666
          Type: Numeric         Width: 1                  Decimals: 0

          User note:  For E1666_1 for previously interviewed respondents, the variable
          indicates whether their parents were alive at the time of the previous
          interview; for new respondents, whether their parents are alive at the time
          of this interview.  For E1666_2 for previously interviewed respondents who
          have a new spouse or partner as of this wave, and whose spouse or partner,
          if any, at the time of the previous interview is not dead, the variable
          indicates whether the parents of their current spouse or partner are alive
          at the time of this interview; for all other previously interviewed
          respondents who have a spouse or partner at the time of this interview, the
          variable indicates whether the parents of their spouse or partner at time of
          their previous interview were alive at the time of that interview.  However,
          see also E1666_2A.
